III.4.1
The generalized Poisson equation

To obtain the generalized Poisson equation in the Newtonian limit of Herglotz
f(R,T)
gravity we consider the
00
component of Eq. (
III.1
). Noting that
\mathchoice{\Theta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.97226pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.97226pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.9pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{00}}}}{\Theta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.97226pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.97226pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.9pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{00}}}}{\Theta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.97226pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.97226pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.9pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{00}}}}{\Theta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.97226pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.97226pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.9pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{00}}}}=-\Theta=-2\rho
and working to first order in the potential
\Phi
, we find

\displaystyle\Delta\Phi=\frac{1}{2f_{R}}\left(f_{T}+\frac{F}{2}\right)\rho-\frac{2}{3f_{R}}\left(H_{00}-\frac{1}{4}g_{00}H\right).

(60)

In the limiting case of general relativity , where
F=16\pi
,
\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}=1
,
\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}=0
,
\mathchoice{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu\nu}}}}=0
, the standard Poisson equation of Newtonian gravity,
\Delta\Phi=4\pi\rho
, is recovered. In the Newtonian limit with constant
f_{R}
, the Herglotz tensor takes the simple form
\mathchoice{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}=\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}\mathchoice{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}
. Defining an effective coupling constant
\mathchoice{k^{{{2}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{eff}}}}{k^{{{2}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{eff}}}}{k^{{{2}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{eff}}}}{k^{{{2}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{eff}}}}=\frac{\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.02959pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.02959pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80815pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.02959pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.02959pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80815pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.02959pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.02959pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80815pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.02959pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.02959pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80815pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}+8\pi}{2\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.15805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.15805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.91753pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.15805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.15805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.91753pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.15805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.15805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.91753pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.15805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.15805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.91753pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}}
, we obtain, to first order in the Herglotz field

\displaystyle\Delta\Phi=k_{eff}^{2}\rho-\frac{1}{6}\left(1+2\Phi\right)\partial_{i}\lambda^{i}+\frac{2}{3}\lambda^{i}\partial_{i}\Phi.

(61)

Considering spherical symmetry of the Herglotz vector
\mathchoice{\lambda^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\lambda^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=\mathchoice{\lambda^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{\lambda^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{\lambda^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{\lambda^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}=\left(0,\vec{\psi}\right)=(0,\psi(r),0,0)
, the generalized Poisson equation reads

\displaystyle\Delta\Phi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathchoice{k^{{{2}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{eff}}}}{k^{{{2}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{eff}}}}{k^{{{2}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{eff}}}}{k^{{{2}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{eff}}}}\rho-\frac{1}{6}\left(1+2\Phi\right)\nabla\cdot\vec{\psi}+\frac{2}{3}\vec{\psi}\cdot\nabla\Phi

(62)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathchoice{k^{{{2}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{eff}}}}{k^{{{2}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{eff}}}}{k^{{{2}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{eff}}}}{k^{{{2}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{eff}}}}\rho-(1+2\Phi)\frac{1}{6r^{2}}\frac{d}{dr}(r^{2}\psi)+\frac{2}{3}\psi\frac{d\Phi}{dr},

or in the vacuum with
\rho=0
,

\frac{1}{r}\frac{d^{2}}{dr^{2}}(r\Phi)=-(1+2\Phi)\frac{1}{6r^{2}}\frac{d}{dr}(r^{2}\psi)+\frac{2}{3}\psi\frac{d\Phi}{dr}.

(63)

By introducing a new variable
u=r\Phi
, and taking into account that

r\frac{d\Phi}{dr}=\frac{du}{dr}-\frac{u}{r},

(64)

Eq. (
63
) takes the form

\frac{d^{2}u}{dr^{2}}-\frac{2}{3}\psi\frac{du}{dr}+\frac{2}{3}\psi\frac{u}{r}=-\frac{1}{6}\left(r+2u\right)\frac{1}{r^{2}}\frac{d}{dr}\left(r^{2}\psi\right).

(65)

To explore possible modifications, we consider specific functional forms of
\psi(r)
.

(a) In the case
\psi=-\frac{3\psi_{0}}{2}={\rm constant}
, Eq. (
65
) becomes

\frac{d^{2}u}{dr^{2}}+\psi_{0}\frac{du}{dr}-2\psi_{0}\frac{u}{r}=\frac{1}{2}\psi_{0}.

(66)

For large values of
r
we can neglect the third term in Eq. (
66
) proportional to
1/r
. Hence, in the constant
\psi
case we obtain for
u
the equation

\frac{d^{2}u}{dr^{2}}+\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}\frac{du}{dr}=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}},

(67)

giving for the modified Newtonian potential the expression

\Phi(r)=\frac{\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}}{r}+\frac{\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}}{\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}}\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}r}}{r}+\frac{1}{2}.

(68)

The constant term in the potential can be neglected since it does not give any contribution to the gravitational force and by analogy with Newtonian gravity we can choose the constants
\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}=-GM,\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}=0
, or
\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}=0,\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}=-GM\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}
where
M
is the mass of the central object. The first choice of constants gives exactly the Newtonian potential with no modifications and the second choice gives the series expansion

\Phi(r)\approx-\frac{GM}{r}+GM\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}-\frac{GM\psi_{0}^{2}}{2}r+\frac{GM\psi_{0}^{3}}{6}r^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(r\right)^{3}.

(69)

Since the correction terms to the Newtonian potential grow with
r
, we discard this case.

(b) The case
\psi=\frac{3\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}}{2r}
. For this choice of
\psi
, Eq. (
65
) takes the form

\frac{d^{2}u}{dr^{2}}-\frac{\psi_{0}}{r}\frac{du}{dr}+\frac{3}{2}\frac{\psi_{0}}{r^{2}}u=-\frac{\psi_{0}}{4r}.

(70)

The general solution of the above equation can be given as

\displaystyle u(r)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{1}r^{\frac{1}{2}\ \left(\psi_{0}-\sqrt{\psi_{0}+\frac{1}{\psi_{0}}-4}\sqrt{\psi_{0}}+1\right)}

(71)

\displaystyle+C_{2}r^{\frac{1}{2}\ \left(\psi_{0}+\sqrt{\psi_{0}+\frac{1}{\psi_{0}}-4}\sqrt{\psi_{0}}+1\right)}-\frac{r}{2}.

Since the Newtonian term
\Phi\approx GM/r
cannot be reproduced from the above solution for any real value of
\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}
we will discard this case.

(c) For
\psi=\frac{3\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}}{2r^{2}}
, Eq. (
65
) becomes

\frac{d^{2}u}{dr^{2}}-\frac{\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}}{r^{2}}\frac{du}{dr}+\frac{\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}}{r^{3}}u=0,

(72)

leading to

\Phi(r)=\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}}}}+\frac{\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}}{\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}}e^{-\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}/r}.

(73)

In principle, it is possible to neglect the
C_{1}
integration constant, as it is not physically measurable. However, for mathematical consistency, we keep the constant and take the series expansion of the potential for large values of
r
, obtaining

\Phi(r)=C_{1}+\frac{C_{2}}{\psi_{0}}+C_{2}\left[-\frac{1}{r}+\frac{\psi_{0}}{2r^{2}}-\frac{\psi_{0}^{2}}{6r^{3}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{r}\right)^{4}\right].

(74)

From Eq. (
74
) it follows immediately that
\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}}}}=GM
and
C_{1}=-\frac{GM}{\psi_{0}}
leads to the correct first order Newtonian approximation, and hence we get the corrected Newtonian potential up to fourth order as

\Phi(r)=-\frac{GM}{r}+\frac{GM\psi_{0}}{2}\frac{1}{r^{2}}-\frac{GM\psi_{0}^{2}}{6}\frac{1}{r^{3}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{r}\right)^{4}.

(75)

III.4.2
The equation of motion of massive particles

To find the equation of motion for a massive test particle we can evaluate Eq. (
53
) in the Newtonian limit of the gravitating system. In this case
\mathchoice{D^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{D^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{D^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{D^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\rightarrow\mathchoice{\nabla^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\nabla^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\nabla^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\nabla^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
and
\mathchoice{h^{{{\mu i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.6406pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.31471pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{{\mu i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.6406pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.31471pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{{\mu i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.6406pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.31471pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{{\mu i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.6406pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.31471pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\rightarrow\mathchoice{g^{{{\mu i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.6406pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.31471pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{{\mu i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.6406pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.31471pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{{\mu i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.6406pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.31471pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{{\mu i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.6916pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.6406pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.31471pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
so that Eq. (
56
) reads

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathcal{F}^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}

\displaystyle=

(78)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}\Delta\Phi}{\left(8\pi+\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}\right)\rho}\vec{\psi}+\frac{2\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}}{\left(8\pi+\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}\right)\rho}\nabla\Phi\cdot\vec{\nabla}\vec{\psi}.

Equivalently, in spherical symmetry this becomes

\displaystyle\mathchoice{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}\hat{r}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}}{2\left(8\pi+\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}\right)\rho}\frac{d\rho}{dr}\hat{r}

(79)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}}{(8\pi+\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}})\rho}\left(3\psi\frac{d^{2}\Phi}{dr^{2}}+\frac{2}{r}\frac{d\Phi}{dr}\frac{d}{dr}(r\psi)\right)\hat{r}.

Alternatively, once the modified potential
\Phi
is known, the additional acceleration can be obtained directly from the deviation of
\Phi
relative to the Newtonian form. For the potential given in Eq. (
75
), we get

\displaystyle\nabla\Phi=\frac{GM}{r^{2}}-\frac{GM\psi_{0}}{r^{3}}+\frac{GM\psi_{0}^{2}}{2r^{4}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{r}\right)^{5},

(80)

from which we approximate the extra acceleration as

\mathchoice{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}=\frac{GM\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}}{r^{3}}\left(\frac{\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}}{2r}-1\right).

(81)

III.4.3
The perihelion precession

Using the expression for the additional acceleration, we can estimate its contributions to the perihelion precession of planets as well as to the deflection of light. For a system with central mass
M
the Laplace-Runge-Lenz vector (LRL) is given by
18

\vec{A}=\vec{v}\times\vec{L}-\epsilon GM\hat{r},

(82)

where
\vec{v}
is the velocity and
\vec{L}
is the specific angular momentum. Also,
\epsilon
parametrizes time-like and null geodesics, hence
\epsilon=1
for massive particles and
\epsilon=0
for light. Classically, the LRL vector is conserved in a central potential
V(r)
, thus the change in perihelion precession
\Delta\phi
over one orbit can be obtained by the derivative of
\vec{A}
with respect to the polar angle
\theta
,

\frac{d\vec{A}}{d\theta}=\frac{d\vec{A}}{dt}\left(\frac{d\theta}{dt}\right)^{-1}=r^{2}\left(\frac{\partial V(r)}{\partial r}+\mathchoice{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}-\frac{\epsilon GM}{r^{2}}\right)\hat{\theta},

(83)

where
\mathchoice{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}
is the magnitude of the extra acceleration. If we consider a general LRL vector
\vec{A}=A\hat{x}
, we arrive at the relation

\frac{1}{r}=\frac{1}{L^{2}}\left(\epsilon GM+A\cos\theta\right).

(84)

Then by using the rate of change of direction for any vector

\vec{\omega}=\frac{\vec{A}\times\dot{\vec{A}}}{A^{2}},

(85)

and the post-Newtonian (Schwarzchild) potential
20

\displaystyle V(r)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\epsilon GM}{r}-\frac{GML^{2}}{r^{3}},

(86)

we obtain for the shift in the massive case (
\epsilon=1
)

\displaystyle\Delta\phi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\omega\,dt

(87)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\frac{1}{A^{2}}\left|\vec{A}\times\frac{d\vec{A}}{d\theta}\right|d\theta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{A}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\left(\frac{3GML^{2}}{r^{2}}+r^{2}\mathchoice{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}\right)\left|\hat{x}\times\hat{\theta}\right|\,d\theta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{6\pi GM}{a(1-e^{2})}+\frac{L^{4}}{A}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\frac{\mathchoice{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}\cos\theta\,d\theta}{(GM+A\cos\theta)^{2}}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.61096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4364pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{GR}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.61096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4364pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{GR}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.61096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4364pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{GR}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.61096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4364pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{GR}}}}+\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}},

since
GM/L^{2}=1/a(1-e^{2})
for eccentricity
e
and semi-major axis
a
.

We can put bounds on the Herglotz field by considering Eq. (
81
) so that

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{L^{4}}{A}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\frac{\mathchoice{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}\cos\theta\,d\theta}{(GM+A\cos\theta)^{2}}

(88)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{GM\psi_{0}}{A}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\bigg(\frac{\psi_{0}}{2L^{4}}(GM+A\cos\theta)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt-\frac{1}{L^{2}}(GM+A\cos\theta)\bigg)\cos\theta d\theta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi\psi_{0}^{2}}{a^{2}(1-e^{2})^{2}}-\frac{\pi\psi_{0}}{a(1-e^{2})}.

Then, for the planet Mercury we have
a=57.91\times 10^{11}
cm and
e=0.205615
, which means that given the observed value of the precession
\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.7749pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.83922pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{obs}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.7749pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.83922pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{obs}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.7749pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.83922pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{obs}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.7749pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.83922pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{obs}}}}=43.11\pm 0.21
arcsec per century
40
, any additional precession must obey
\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}\leq\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.7749pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.83922pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{obs}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.7749pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.83922pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{obs}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.7749pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.83922pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{obs}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.2382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.7749pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.83922pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{obs}}}}-\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.61096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4364pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{GR}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.61096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4364pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{GR}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.61096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4364pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{GR}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.61096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4364pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{GR}}}}=0.1^{+0.2}_{-0.1}
arcsec per century. If we take the the extra precession to contain the entire deviation from observation then this gives an upper bound of
\mathchoice{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}{\psi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}}}}\approx 5.5\times 10^{12}
cm for the case of an inverse square Herglotz function.

III.4.4
The deflection of light

To calculate the deflection of light we take
\epsilon=0
and consider the deflection bounds
-\pi/2
to
\pi/2
. Doing this gives

\displaystyle\Delta\phi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{-\pi/2}^{\pi/2}\omega\,dt

(89)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{-\pi/2}^{\pi/2}\left[\frac{3GMA\cos^{2}\theta}{L^{2}}+\frac{L^{4}\mathchoice{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}}{A^{3}\cos^{2}\theta}\right]\cos\theta\,d\theta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4GM}{b}+\frac{b^{2}}{A}\int_{-\pi/2}^{\pi/2}\frac{\mathchoice{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{a^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}}{\cos\theta}d\theta,

by defining the impact parameter
b=L^{2}/A
. Looking at Eq. (
89
) we see that the extra deflection of photons depends on the magnitude of the LRL vector. In the case of photons we will assume that they have an effective mass given by
m_{eff}=h\nu/c^{2}
, where
h
is Planck’s constant, and
\nu
is the frequency, a relation which is obtained by assuming that the photon energy
E=h\nu
is related to an effective mass via Einstein’s relation
E=m_{eff}c^{2}
. Thus for the magnitude of the Laplace-Runge-Lenz vector we obtain
A=h_{p}^{2}\nu^{2}b
where
h_{p}=h/m_{p}
is the Planck constant divided by the Planck mass
m_{p}
. Then using Eq. (
81
) we find

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{b^{2}GM\psi_{0}}{A}\int_{-\pi/2}^{\pi/2}\left(\frac{\psi_{0}}{2b^{4}}\cos^{3}\theta-\frac{1}{b^{3}}\cos^{2}\theta\right)d\theta

(90)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2GM\psi_{0}^{2}}{3Ab^{2}}-\frac{\pi GM\psi_{0}}{2Ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{GM\psi_{0}}{h_{p}^{2}\nu^{2}b^{2}}\left(\frac{2\psi_{0}}{3b}-\frac{\pi}{2}\right).

Thus, we find that in the presence of the Herglotz field, the bending of light depends on the frequency. In particular, the magnitude of the bending is proportional to the square of the wavelength. Interestingly enough, this result matches the scaling proposed by light traveling through a plasma medium
81
;
25
;
70
.

For the specific case of impact parameter being equivalent to the solar radius,
b=R_{\odot}
, we find

\frac{\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{p}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{p}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{p}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{p}}}}}{\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.61096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4364pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{GR}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.61096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4364pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{GR}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.61096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4364pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{GR}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.28607pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.61096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.4364pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{GR}}}}}=1.02\times 10^{-7}\left(\frac{\lambda}{\mu\text{m}}\right)^{2},

(91)

where
\lambda
is the wavelength of the light and
\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{p}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{p}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{p}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{p}}}}
is the so called plasma deflection angle predicted in
81
which agrees with recent observations using the Cassini spacecraft
13
. Using
\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{p}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{p}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{p}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{p}}}}=\mathchoice{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}{\Delta\phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{E}}}}
and the above relation we find

\frac{\psi_{0}}{2h_{p}^{2}b}\left(\frac{\psi_{0}}{3b}-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)=1.02\times 10^{-7}\left(\frac{1}{\mu\text{m}}\right)^{2},

(92)

when considering Eq. (
90
). Taking the positive solution we find
\psi_{0}\approx 1.6\times 10^{11}
cm. This value falls within the upper bound of that calculated via the perihelion shift of Mercury, and is only one order of magnitude short of the assumption that the Herglotz field accounts for the entire precession difference not described by GR.

IV
Cosmological applications

To explore the cosmological implications of Herglotz-type
f(R,T)
gravity, we assume an isotropic and homogeneous flat Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) metric of the form

\displaystyle ds^{2}=-dt^{2}+a^{2}(t)\delta_{ij}dx^{i}dx^{j},

(93)

where
a(t)
is the scale factor and
t
denotes the comoving time. By spatial homogeneity, the Herglotz field is constrained to the form

\displaystyle\lambda_{\mu}=(\phi(t),0,0,0),

(94)

with
\phi(t)
a smooth function of the cosmic time. Similarly, we must have that
f(R,T)
and its subsequent derivatives only depend on
t
. Finally, we model the matter content as a perfect fluid with pressure
p
and energy density
\rho
,

\displaystyle T_{\mu\nu}=(p+\rho)u_{\mu}u_{\nu}+pg_{\mu\nu},

(95)

where the fluid four-velocity obeys
u_{\mu}u^{\mu}=-1
. For the matter Lagrangian, we adopt the common choice
\mathcal{L}_{m}=p
.

IV.1
The generalized Friedmann equations

By taking the typical value
F=16\pi
, the FLRW equations for Herglotz-type
f(R,T)
gravity read

\displaystyle\begin{split}&3f_{R}\dot{H}+3\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}H^{2}+3\left(\phi\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}-\mathchoice{\dot{f}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{\dot{f}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{\dot{f}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}{\dot{f}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.06009pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.75835pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.68454pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{R}}}}\right)H-\frac{1}{2}f\\
&=-\mathchoice{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}{f^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.79965pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.54399pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53142pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{T}}}}(\rho+p)-8\pi\rho,\\
\end{split}

(96)

Physically,
\Gamma
can be interpreted as the rate of energy creation or annihilation in the cosmic environment, arising from the dissipative effects associated with the geometry-matter coupling and the Herglotz field.

As an indicator of the dynamical evolution of the Universe we introduce the deceleration parameter
q
, defined as

q=\frac{d}{dt}\frac{1}{H}-1.

(109)

With the use of Eqs. (
106
) and (
102
) the deceleration parameter can be expressed as

q=\frac{3}{2}\frac{\rho+p+\mathchoice{\rho^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{eff}}}}{\rho^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{eff}}}}{\rho^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{eff}}}}{\rho^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{eff}}}}+\mathchoice{p^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{eff}}}}{p^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{eff}}}}{p^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{eff}}}}{p^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{eff}}}}}{\rho+\mathchoice{\rho^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81026pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{eff}}}}{\rho^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.16228pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.13435pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.81
